# Commands · I · part 3

1,448 entries, init-mcp-server to inversion. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **init-mcp-server**: Scaffold a standalone MCP (Model Context Protocol) server project. Supports Python (official mcp SDK) and TypeScript (@modelcontextprotocol/sdk). Use this for pure MCP servers (Slack-MCP, Postgres-MCP, GitHub-MCP, etc.) — for MCP servers embedded inside a SaaS app, use /auto-build or /retrofit instead. Usage: /init-mcp-server &lt;server-name&gt; [--lang=python|typescript] [--transport=stdio|http|sse] [--with-tools] [--with-prompts] [--with-resources] ([alphaaiservice/cortex](https://github.com/alphaaiservice/cortex/blob/HEAD/commands/init-mcp-server.md) · ★1)

- **init-plane**: Set up a local self-hosted Plane (makeplane) instance and wire pbrain to it. Plane is pbrain's sole project brain (Module → Issue → Sub-issue + a Linear-like UI); pbrain's /plan-my-work and /end-of-day then read ready tasks from Plane and write status back. Guided, idempotent — safe to re-run. Use when the user wants to run Plane locally as pbrain's task tracker (task planning + project progress require Plane). ([baymac/pbrain](https://github.com/baymac/pbrain/blob/HEAD/commands/init-plane.md) · ★1)

- **init-project**: Deep project-initialization command. Phase 0 parses arguments, prints a plain-language primer, branches on an existing `CLAUDE.md` (review and improve / leave it / start fresh), opens a typed preference queue, and runs the gitignore guard for `CLAUDE.local.md`. Phase 1 spawns four parallel `ac:explore` agents (manifest plus build, languages plus frameworks, conventions plus style, existing agent infra), each brief carrying its own DEPTH and BUDGET line. Phase 2 runs the gap-fill interview one question per call (scope, commands, golden rules, testing, off-limits, branch flow) and routes every… ([anilcancakir/claude-code](https://github.com/anilcancakir/claude-code/blob/HEAD/plugins/ac/commands/init-project.md) · ★3)

- **intake**: Vendor-agnostic batch scanner for Ready queues. Notion PRD database URL → finds Status=Ready PRDs and runs lisa-plan per item.
